### [SFR-AFFINITY-DESKTOP-DELTA.gaps] Residual Gaps

```yaml
gaps:
  - id: "affinity-desktop-delta-gap-001"
    detail: "Desktop XML table-of-contents files remain blocked by 403, so index.html/Jina snapshots are the current machine evidence."
    mitigation: "If exact XML parity is required, capture desktop contents.xml through an authenticated/browser session and diff it against this file."
  - id: "affinity-desktop-delta-gap-002"
    detail: "Desktop-only delta is path-based; pages with same relative path but different desktop-vs-iPad behavior still require page-level inspection."
    mitigation: "Promote implementation candidates through command contracts and inspect page behavior before coding."
```
